00273: def owner_meshes(slot):
00274:     root = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00275:     if root is None or root.type != 'EMPTY':
00276:         candidates = [o for o in bpy.data.objects
00277:                       if o.type == 'EMPTY' and o.name.split('.')[0] == slot]
00278:         assert candidates, f"Missing semantic marker: {slot}"
00279:         root = candidates[0]
00280:     meshes = [o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00281:     assert meshes, f"No mesh descendants: {slot}"
00282:     return meshes
00283: 
00284: def customize(slot,metallic=None,roughness=None,texture=None):
00285:     for obj in owner_meshes(slot):
00286:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00287:         for idx,original in enumerate(list(obj.data.materials)):
00288:             if original is None:
00289:                 continue
00290:             mat = original.copy()
00291:             obj.data.materials[idx] = mat
00292:             mat.use_nodes = True
00293:             nodes,links = mat.node_tree.nodes,mat.node_tree.links
00294:             bs = next((n for n in nodes if n.type == 'BSDF_PRINCIPLED'),None)
00295:             if bs is None:
00296:                 continue
00297:             if metallic is not None:
00298:                 bs.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00299:             if roughness is not None:
00300:                 bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00301:             if texture == "stone":
00302:                 noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00303:                 noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 185
00304:                 noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2.5
00305:                 ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00306:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .22
00307:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (.065,.061,.047,1)
00308:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .77
00309:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (.57,.53,.42,1)
00310:                 middle = ramp.color_ramp.elements.new(.51)
00311:                 middle.color = (.255,.24,.19,1)
00312:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00313:                 links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00314:                 bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00315:                 bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .25
00316:                 bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .006
00317:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])

00318: def owner_meshes(slot):
00319:     root = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00320:     if root is None or root.type != 'EMPTY':
00321:         candidates = [o for o in bpy.data.objects
00322:                       if o.type == 'EMPTY' and o.name.split('.')[0] == slot]
00323:         assert candidates, f"Missing semantic marker: {slot}"
00324:         root = candidates[0]
00325:     meshes = [o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00326:     assert meshes, f"No mesh descendants: {slot}"
00327:     return meshes
00328: 
00329: def customize(slot,metallic=None,roughness=None,texture=None):
00330:     for obj in owner_meshes(slot):
00331:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00332:         for idx,original in enumerate(list(obj.data.materials)):
00333:             if original is None:
00334:                 continue
00335:             mat = original.copy()
00336:             obj.data.materials[idx] = mat
00337:             mat.use_nodes = True
00338:             nodes,links = mat.node_tree.nodes,mat.node_tree.links
00339:             bs = next((n for n in nodes if n.type == 'BSDF_PRINCIPLED'),None)
00340:             if bs is None:
00341:                 continue
00342:             if metallic is not None:
00343:                 bs.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00344:             if roughness is not None:
00345:                 bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00346:             if texture == "stone":
00347:                 noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00348:                 noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 185
00349:                 noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2.5
00350:                 ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00351:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .22
00352:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (.065,.061,.047,1)
00353:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .77
00354:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (.57,.53,.42,1)
00355:                 middle = ramp.color_ramp.elements.new(.51)
00356:                 middle.color = (.255,.24,.19,1)
00357:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00358:                 links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00359:                 bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00360:                 bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .25
00361:                 bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .006
00362:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00363:                 links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00364:             elif texture == "fabric":
00365:                 noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00366:                 noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 220
00367:                 noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00368:                 bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00369:                 bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .20
00370:                 bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .002
00371:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])

00157: def descendants(marker):
00158:     found = bpy.data.objects.get(marker)
00159:     assert found is not None, marker
00160:     meshes = [o for o in found.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00161:     assert meshes, marker

00159: def descendants(marker):

00162:     meshes = [o for o in found.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
